<div class="block__inner">
<div class="block__content">
<h4>Connect with the APSC</h4>
<ul class="social-icon">
<li><a href="https://www.facebook.com/AusPublicService/"><i aria-hidden="true" class="fab fa-facebook-square fa-3x"> </i></a></li>
<li><a href="https://twitter.com/PublicServiceAU"><i aria-hidden="true" class="fab fa-twitter-square fa-3x"> </i></a></li>
<li><a href="https://www.youtube.com/channel/UCFqXOCf11V-OuKsUtBWH3jg"><i aria-hidden="true" class="fab fa-youtube-square fa-3x"> </i></a></li>
<li><a href="https://au.linkedin.com/company/apsc"><i aria-hidden="true" class="fab fa-linkedin fa-3x"> </i></a></li>
</ul>
<p>The Australian Public Service Commission (APSC) is a central agency within the Prime Minister and Cabinet portfolio. The Commission supports two statutory office holders: the Australian Public Service Commissioner - who is also agency head - and the Merit Protection Commissioner. Their functions are set out in sections 41(1) and 50(1), respectively, of the Public Service Act 1999.</p>
<p> </p>
